diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/aggregate_facts.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/aggregate_facts.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..10002e01ee
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/aggregate_facts.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,166 @@
+//! Exact non-empty facts established by successful count guards.
+
+use super::*;
+use std::collections::BTreeSet;
+
+pub(super) const NONEMPTY: &str = "$aggregateNonempty";
+/// Capture marker for a local whose current value is an exact `count(domain)`
+/// observation. It lives on the result and every domain root: ordinary
+/// rebinding, a write, an authority edge, or a control-flow meet therefore
+/// destroys stale provenance without repurposing diagnostic `origins`.
+pub(super) const COUNT_CAPTURE: &str = "$aggregateCountCapture";
+
+impl Binder<'_> {
+    /// A non-empty observation survives evaluation of its guard only when the
+    /// complete condition is write-free. Unlike point facts, aggregate facts
+    /// deliberately admit filters and seeks because those are part of the
+    /// exact domain identity recorded below.
+    pub(super) fn aggregate_guard_safe(&self, value: &A) -> bool {
+        match value {
+            A::Name { .. } | A::Focal { .. } | A::Literal { .. } => true,
+            A::Array { items, .. } => items.iter().all(|item| self.aggregate_guard_safe(item)),
+            A::Object { fields, .. } => fields.iter().all(|field| field.value.as_ref().is_none_or(|value| self.aggregate_guard_safe(value))),
+            A::Unary { value, .. } | A::Assert { value, .. } | A::ShapeUse { base: value, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(value),
+            A::Binary { left, right, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(left) && self.aggregate_guard_safe(right),
+            A::Member { base, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(base),
+            A::Call { name, args, .. } => self.catalog.functions.get(name).is_none_or(|function|
+                self.pure_functions.get(&function.name) == Some(&true))
+                && args.iter().all(|arg| self.aggregate_guard_safe(arg)),
+            A::Read(read) | A::Exists { read, .. } | A::Created { read, .. } => {
+                read.computed.values().all(|value| self.aggregate_guard_safe(value))
+                    && read.keys.iter().all(|key| self.aggregate_guard_safe(&key.value))
+                    && read.seeks.iter().all(|seek| self.aggregate_guard_safe(&seek.value))
+                    && read.filter.as_deref().is_none_or(|filter| self.aggregate_guard_safe(filter))
+                    && read.matching.as_deref().is_none_or(|matching| self.aggregate_guard_safe(matching))
+                    && read.range.as_ref().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_deref()).is_none_or(|cursor| self.aggregate_guard_safe(cursor))
+            }
+            A::Shape { base, fields, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(base)
+                && fields.iter().all(|field| match field {
+                    ShapeField::Field { value, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(value),
+                    ShapeField::Spread { .. } | ShapeField::NamedSpread { .. } | ShapeField::Bare { .. } => true,
+                }),
+            A::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(then)
+                && self.aggregate_guard_safe(condition)
+                && self.aggregate_guard_safe(otherwise),
+            A::Merge(_) | A::Walk { .. } | A::Write { .. } => false,
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Record only implications that follow from count's nonnegative range.
+    /// The common authored form is `require n > 0`, where `n` was bound from
+    /// `count(domain)`; direct count calls use the same identity.
+    // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#4-non-empty-domain-proof — the fact belongs to the exact domain roots and transaction state.
+    pub(super) fn record_aggregate_nonempty(&mut self, condition: &A, truth: bool) {
+        if !truth { return; }
+        let A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = condition else { return };
+        let (candidate, threshold, op) = if let Some(n) = literal_int(right) {
+            (left.as_ref(), n, op.as_str())
+        } else if let Some(n) = literal_int(left) {
+            let reversed = match op.as_str() { "<" => ">", "<=" => ">=", ">" => "<", ">=" => "<=", other => other };
+            (right.as_ref(), n, reversed)
+        } else { return };
+        let proves_positive = match op {
+            ">" => threshold >= 0,
+            ">=" | "=" => threshold >= 1,
+            "!=" => threshold == 0,
+            _ => false,
+        };
+        if !proves_positive { return; }
+        let Some((key, roots)) = self.count_key(candidate) else { return };
+        for root in roots {
+            let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(&root).cloned() else { return };
+            binding.fields.insert(key.clone(), Ty::Bool);
+            self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(root, binding);
+        }
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn aggregate_nonempty(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
+        self.aggregate_key(read).is_some_and(|(key, roots)| roots.iter().all(|root|
+            self.binding(root).is_some_and(|binding| binding.fields.get(&key) == Some(&Ty::Bool))))
+    }
+
+    /// Resolve count provenance before rebinding `name`: a count domain may
+    /// itself refer to the old value of that local. Such a self-dependent
+    /// capture cannot be represented by the post-assignment binding and is
+    /// therefore deliberately not narrowed.
+    pub(super) fn count_capture(&self, name: &str, value: &A) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
+        let A::Call { name: call, args, .. } = value else { return None };
+        if call != "count" || args.len() != 1 || self.catalog.functions.contains_key(call)
+            || !self.aggregate_guard_safe(value) { return None; }
+        let read = args.first().and_then(|arg| self.aggregate_domain(arg))?;
+        let (aggregate, roots) = self.aggregate_key(&read)?;
+        if roots.contains(name) { return None; }
+        let capture = vec![COUNT_CAPTURE.into(), aggregate[1].clone()];
+        Some((capture, roots))
+    }
+
+    /// Attach pre-resolved capture-time provenance after assigning `name`.
+    /// `origins` remains diagnostic history; this field fact is the reaching
+    /// definition proof used by a later `require name > 0`.
+    pub(super) fn record_count_capture(&mut self, name: &str, capture: Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)>) {
+        let Some((capture, mut owners)) = capture else { return };
+        owners.insert(name.to_owned());
+        for owner in owners {
+            let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(&owner).cloned() else { return };
+            binding.fields.insert(capture.clone(), Ty::Bool);
+            self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(owner, binding);
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn count_key(&self, value: &A) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
+        let (count_arg, captured_name) = match value {
+            A::Call { name, args, .. } if name == "count" && args.len() == 1
+                && !self.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) => args.first(),
+            A::Name { name, .. } => self.binding(name)?.origins.iter().rev().find_map(|(_, origin)| match origin {
+                A::Call { name, args, .. } if name == "count" && args.len() == 1
+                    && !self.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) => args.first(),
+                _ => None,
+            }),
+            _ => None,
+        }.map(|arg| (arg, match value { A::Name { name, .. } => Some(name.as_str()), _ => None }))?;
+        let result = self.aggregate_key(&self.aggregate_domain(count_arg)?)?;
+        if let Some(name) = captured_name {
+            let capture = vec![COUNT_CAPTURE.into(), result.0[1].clone()];
+            if self.binding(name)?.fields.get(&capture) != Some(&Ty::Bool)
+                || !result.1.iter().all(|root| self.binding(root)
+                    .is_some_and(|binding| binding.fields.get(&capture) == Some(&Ty::Bool)))
+            {
+                return None;
+            }
+        }
+        Some(result)
+    }
+
+    fn aggregate_key(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
+        if read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() { return None; }
+        let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
+        let keys = read.keys.iter().map(|key| Some(format!("{:?}:{}", key.column,
+            self.aggregate_value(&key.value, &mut roots)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
+        let seeks = read.seeks.iter().map(|seek| Some(format!("{}:{}:{}", seek.column, seek.op,
+            self.aggregate_value(&seek.value, &mut roots)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
+        let filter = match read.filter.as_deref() {
+            Some(value) => Some(self.aggregate_value(value, &mut roots)?),
+            None => None,
+        };
+        if roots.is_empty() { roots.insert(pure_calls::CONSTANTS.into()); }
+        let identity = format!("{:?}:{:?}:{:?}:{keys:?}:{seeks:?}:{filter:?}", read.table, read.index, read.order_keys);
+        Some((vec![NONEMPTY.into(), identity], roots))
+    }
+
+    fn aggregate_value(&self, value: &A, roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<String> {
+        match value {
+            A::Focal { .. } => Some("focal".into()),
+            A::Member { base, name, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. }) => Some(format!("focal.{name}")),
+            A::Literal { .. } | A::Name { .. } | A::Member { .. } => self.stable_value(value, roots).map(|value| value.key()),
+            A::Unary { op, value, .. } => Some(format!("unary:{op}:{}", self.aggregate_value(value, roots)?)),
+            A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => Some(format!("binary:{op}:{}:{}", self.aggregate_value(left, roots)?, self.aggregate_value(right, roots)?)),
+            A::Call { name, args, .. } => {
+                let function = self.catalog.functions.get(name)?;
+                if self.pure_functions.get(&function.name) != Some(&true) { return None; }
+                Some(format!("call:{name}:{:?}", args.iter().map(|arg| self.aggregate_value(arg, roots)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?))
+            }
+            A::Array { items, .. } => Some(format!("array:{:?}", items.iter().map(|item| self.aggregate_value(item, roots)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?)),
+            _ => None,
+        }
+    }
+}

@@ -462,6 +464,14 @@ impl Binder<'_> {
 
     /// After `require x != null` or inside `if x != null:`, `x` is present.
     pub(super) fn narrow(&mut self, condition: &A, then_branch: bool) {
+        // Aggregate facts may describe filtered domains, so they have a
+        // slightly wider write-free admission check than point facts. Record
+        // them once for the whole, already-evaluated condition: recursively
+        // recording a safe left operand would be stale when a later operand
+        // calls a writeful helper.
+        if self.aggregate_guard_safe(condition) {
+            self.record_aggregate_nonempty(condition, then_branch);
+        }
         self.narrow_inner(condition, then_branch, self.point_guard_safe(condition));
     }

     8	pub(super) enum Stable {
     9	    Literal(String),
    10	    Snapshot(String, Vec<String>),
    11	    Viewer,
    12	    Call(String, Vec<Stable>),

    15	impl Binder<'_> {
    16	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.2 — a repeated pure call narrows like a snapshot field.
    17	    // Facts live on *every* argument root so the existing branch intersections,
    18	    // reassignment invalidation and loop fixed point apply to them as to fields.
    19	    fn stable_call(&self, value: &A) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
    20	        if !matches!(value, A::Call { .. }) { return None; }
    21	        let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
    22	        let stable = self.stable_value(value, &mut roots)?;
    23	        if roots.is_empty() { roots.insert(CONSTANTS.into()); }
    24	        Some((vec!["$pureCall".into(), stable.key()], roots))

    26	
    27	    pub(super) fn stable_value(&self, value: &A, roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<Stable> {
    28	        match value {
    29	            A::Literal { .. } => Some(Stable::Literal(print::expression(value))),
    30	            A::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer" => Some(Stable::Viewer),
    31	            A::Name { name, .. } if matches!(name.as_str(), "now" | "commit" | "new") => None,
    32	            A::Name { .. } | A::Member { .. } | A::Focal { .. } => {
    33	                let (root, path) = control::field_path(value, self.focal.as_ref().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()))?;
    34	                let runtime = if let Some(binding) = self.binding(&root) {
    35	                    roots.insert(root.clone());
    36	                    binding.runtime.clone()
    37	                } else {
    38	                    // A shape's implicit row is an immutable lexical snapshot.
    39	                    // Its unique runtime identity separates projection scopes;
    40	                    // with no mutable argument roots, use the constant anchor.
    41	                    self.focal.as_ref().filter(|(name, _)| name == &root)?.0.clone()
    42	                };
    43	                Some(Stable::Snapshot(runtime, path))
    44	            }

    47	                if !self.pure_functions.get(&function.name).copied().unwrap_or(false) { return None; }

    49	            }
    50	            _ => None,
    51	        }
    52	    }
    53	
    54	    pub(super) fn narrow_call(&mut self, value: &A, present: bool) {

    67	    }
    68	
    69	    pub(super) fn call_refinement(&self, value: &A) -> Option<Ty> {
    70	        let (key, roots) = self.stable_call(value)?;
    71	        let mut result = None;
    72	        for root in roots {

    94	// Inspect expanded IR, after argument setup: this covers transitive helpers,

    97	pub(super) fn expression(value: &Expr) -> bool {

     3	use super::*;

     5	impl Binder<'_> {
     6	    // ----- statements -----
     7	
     8	    pub(super) fn block(&mut self, statements: &[S], out: &mut setup::Body, completes: bool) -> Fallible<()> {
     9	        let before = self.visible_bindings();
    10	        self.push_scope();
    11	        let count = statements.len();
    12	        let mut result = Ok(());
    13	        for (index, statement) in statements.iter().enumerate() {
    14	            if let Err(error) = self.statement(statement, out, completes && index + 1 == count) {
    15	                if result.is_ok() { result = Err(error); }
    16	            }
    17	        }
    18	        let after = self.visible_bindings();
    19	        self.pop_scope();
    20	        for (name, mut old) in before {
    21	            let new = &after[&name];
    22	            if old.runtime == new.runtime && old.revision != new.revision {
    23	                self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name, new.clone());
    24	            } else if old.runtime == new.runtime {
    25	                // Facts added inside a lexical block remain local, while a

    27	                let surviving_fields: BTreeMap<_, _> = old.fields.iter()

    29	                    .map(|(path, ty)| (path.clone(), ty.clone()))
    30	                    .collect();
    31	                if surviving_fields.len() != old.fields.len() {
    32	                    old.fields = surviving_fields;
    33	                    self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name, old);
    34	                }
    35	            }
    36	        }
    37	        result
    38	    }

    40	    pub(super) fn append_errors(&mut self, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {

    50	    pub(super) fn statement(&mut self, statement: &S, out: &mut setup::Body, completes: bool) -> Fallible<()> {

    52	        if let Err(error) = &result {

     9	    pub(super) fn point_guard_safe(&self, value: &A) -> bool {

    20	    fn point_key(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {

   176	fn rule_probes(rule: &snapback4_core::schema::RuleExpr, target: &str) -> bool {

    97	    /// Facts may prove a read rule without restating the whole expression:
    98	    /// one admitted disjunct proves an `or`, while every conjunct proves an
    99	    /// `and`. This is the same propositional narrowing used by control flow.
   100	    pub(super) fn authorization_proved(&self, condition: &A, truth: bool) -> bool {
   101	        if self.condition_known(condition, truth) || self.authorization_holds(condition, truth) {
   102	            return true;
   103	        }
   104	        match condition {
   105	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => self.authorization_proved(value, !truth),
   106	            A::Binary {
   107	                op, left, right, ..
   108	            } if op == "and" && truth => {
   109	                self.authorization_proved(left, true) && self.authorization_proved(right, true)
   110	            }
   111	            A::Binary {
   112	                op, left, right, ..
   113	            } if op == "and" => {
   114	                self.authorization_proved(left, false) || self.authorization_proved(right, false)
   115	            }
   116	            A::Binary {
   117	                op, left, right, ..
   118	            } if op == "or" && truth => {
   119	                self.authorization_proved(left, true) || self.authorization_proved(right, true)
   120	            }
   121	            A::Binary {
   122	                op, left, right, ..
   123	            } if op == "or" => {
   124	                self.authorization_proved(left, false) && self.authorization_proved(right, false)
   125	            }
   126	            _ => false,
   127	        }
   128	    }
   129	
   130	    fn authorization_key(
   131	        &self,
   132	        condition: &A,
   133	        truth: bool,
   134	        domain: AuthorizationDomain,
   135	    ) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
   136	        let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
   137	        let key = self.condition_identity(condition, truth, domain, &mut roots)?;
   138	        if roots.is_empty() {
   139	            roots.insert(pure_calls::CONSTANTS.to_owned());
   140	        }
   141	        Some((vec![AUTHORIZATION.into(), key], roots))
   142	    }
   143	
   144	    fn condition_identity(
   145	        &self,
   146	        condition: &A,
   147	        truth: bool,
   148	        domain: AuthorizationDomain,
   149	        roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>,
   150	    ) -> Option<String> {
   151	        match condition {
   152	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => {
   153	                self.condition_identity(value, !truth, domain, roots)
   154	            }
   155	            A::Exists { read, .. } => {
   156	                let raw = matches!(domain, AuthorizationDomain::Rule)
   157	                    || truth
   158	                    || self.program_absence_is_raw(read);
   159	                let identity = self.read_identity("exists", if raw { "raw" } else { "readable" }, read, roots)?;
   160	                Some(format!("truth:{truth}:{identity}"))
   161	            }
   162	            A::Created { read, .. } => {
   163	                let identity = self.read_identity("created", "raw", read, roots)?;
   164	                Some(format!("truth:{truth}:{identity}"))
   165	            }
   166	            A::Binary {
   167	                op, left, right, ..
   168	            } if matches!(op.as_str(), "=" | "!=") => {
   169	                let mut operands = [
   170	                    self.value_identity(left, domain, roots)?,
   171	                    self.value_identity(right, domain, roots)?,
   172	                ];
   173	                operands.sort();
   174	                let equal = (op == "=") == truth;
   175	                Some(format!("compare:{equal}:{:?}", operands))
   176	            }
   177	            _ => Some(format!(
   178	                "truth:{truth}:{}",
   179	                self.value_identity(condition, domain, roots)?
   180	            )),
   181	        }
   182	    }
   183	
   184	    fn value_identity(&self, value: &A, domain: AuthorizationDomain, roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<String> {
   185	        match value {
   186	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } => {
   187	                Some(format!("unary:{op}:{}", self.value_identity(value, domain, roots)?))
   188	            }
   189	            A::Binary {
   190	                op, left, right, ..
   191	            } => Some(format!(
   192	                "binary:{op}:{}:{}",
   193	                self.value_identity(left, domain, roots)?,
   194	                self.value_identity(right, domain, roots)?
   195	            )),
   196	            // Nested witnesses have no known polarity. Keep their observable
   197	            // program domain separate from the raw witness used by a rule.
   198	            A::Exists { read, .. } => self.read_identity("exists", match domain {
   199	                AuthorizationDomain::Program => "readable",
   200	                AuthorizationDomain::Rule => "raw",
   201	            }, read, roots),
   202	            A::Created { read, .. } => self.read_identity("created", "raw", read, roots),
   203	            _ => self.stable_value(value, roots).map(|value| value.key()),
   204	        }
   205	    }
   206	
   207	    fn read_identity(
   208	        &self,
   209	        kind: &str,
   210	        domain: &str,
   211	        read: &ReadExpr,
   212	        roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>,
   213	    ) -> Option<String> {
   214	        if read.range.is_some()
   215	            || read.matching.is_some()
   216	            || read.filter.is_some()
   217	            || !read.seeks.is_empty()
   218	        {
   219	            return None;
   220	        }
   221	        let keys = read
   222	            .keys
   223	            .iter()
   224	            .map(|key| {
   225	                Some(format!(
   226	                    "{:?}:{}",
   227	                    key.column,
   228	                    self.value_identity(&key.value, AuthorizationDomain::Rule, roots)?
   229	                ))
   230	            })
   231	            .collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
   232	        Some(format!("{kind}:{domain}:{:?}:{:?}:{keys:?}", read.table, read.index))
   233	    }
   234	
   235	    /// A negative program existence test establishes raw absence only when
   236	    /// every row selected by its complete point key is structurally readable.
   237	    fn program_absence_is_raw(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   238	        if self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job && self.viewer_depth.is_none() {
   239	            return true;
   240	        }
   241	        program_absence_is_raw(self.schema, read)
   242	    }
   243	
   244	    fn condition_known(&self, condition: &A, truth: bool) -> bool {
   245	        match condition {
   246	            A::Literal {
   247	                value: Literal::Bool(value),
   248	                ..
   249	            } => *value == truth,
   250	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => self.condition_known(value, !truth),
   251	            A::Binary {
   252	                op, left, right, ..
   253	            } if op == "and" && truth => {
   254	                self.condition_known(left, true) && self.condition_known(right, true)
   255	            }
   256	            A::Binary {
   257	                op, left, right, ..
   258	            } if op == "or" && truth => {
   259	                self.condition_known(left, true) || self.condition_known(right, true)
   260	            }
   261	            A::Binary {
   262	                op, left, right, ..
   263	            } if matches!(op.as_str(), "=" | "!=") => {
   264	                let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
   265	                let same = self
   266	                    .value_identity(left, AuthorizationDomain::Rule, &mut roots)
   267	                    .zip(self.value_identity(right, AuthorizationDomain::Rule, &mut roots))
   268	                    .is_some_and(|(left, right)| left == right);
   269	                same && ((op == "=") == truth)
   270	            }
   271	            _ => false,
   272	        }
   273	    }
   274	}
   275	
   276	/// Whether an authorized program miss is also a raw miss for the complete
   277	/// point key. Shared with the matching authoring note so type and lint facts
   278	/// use one witness-domain boundary.
   279	pub(in crate::bind) fn program_absence_is_raw(
   280	    schema: &snapback4_core::schema::Schema,
   281	    read: &ReadExpr,
   282	) -> bool {
   283	    let Some(table) = schema.table(&read.table) else { return false };
   284	    // A through child inherits its parent's visibility outside the child's
   285	    // compiled rule. A raw rule witness can still see the live child.
   286	    if table.through.is_some() { return false; }
   287	    if table.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable() { return true; }
   288	    if read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() || read.filter.is_some() || !read.seeks.is_empty() {
   289	        return false;
   290	    }

    21	impl Runner<'_> {
    22	    // @ref LLP 3000.022#14.3 — no predicted delta enters receipt-derived state.
    23	    pub(super) fn defer_receipt_maintain(&self, m: &Maintain) -> bool {
    24	        self.device && self.schema.receipt_dependencies.contains_key(&m.target)
    25	    }
    26	
    27	    // Cache ordinary group keys once per target/query. Predicates and receipt
    28	    // fields are never evaluated; unknown/computed keys conservatively freeze
    29	    // the target. An unpinned read is incomplete if any group is dirty, while
    30	    // each returned row still decides independently whether to recount.
    31	    #[inline(never)]
    32	    pub(super) fn receipt_maintain_pending(&mut self, m: &Maintain, group: Option<&[Value]>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    33	        if !self.defer_receipt_maintain(m) { return Ok(false); }
    34	        if !self.receipt_pending_groups.contains_key(&m.target) {
    35	            let fields = if let Some(k) = &m.row_key { vec![k.source_key.clone()] }
    36	                else { m.by.iter().map(|f| m.aliases.get(f).unwrap_or(f).clone()).collect() };
    37	            let mut groups = self.receipt_overlay_groups(&m.source, &fields, !m.computed.is_empty())?;
    38	            if let Some(k) = &m.row_key {
    39	                let member = self.receipt_overlay_groups(&k.table, std::slice::from_ref(&k.member_key), false)?;
    40	                groups = groups.zip(member).map(|(mut source, member)| { source.extend(member); source });
    41	            }
    42	            self.receipt_pending_groups.insert(m.target.clone(), groups);
    43	        }
    44	        let dirty = self.receipt_pending_groups[&m.target].as_ref().is_none_or(|keys|
    45	            group.map_or(!keys.is_empty(), |group| keys.iter().any(|key| key == group)));
    46	        if dirty {
    47	            self.value_source_incomplete = true;
    48	            self.components.note(|frame| frame.pending = true);
    49	        }
    50	        Ok(dirty)
    51	    }
    52	
    53	    fn receipt_overlay_groups(&self, table: &str, fields: &[String], computed: bool) -> Result<Option<Vec<Vec<Value>>>, RuntimeError> {
    54	        if !self.tx.has_provisional(table)? { return Ok(Some(Vec::new())); }
    55	        if computed || fields.iter().any(|f| self.schema.receipt_dependencies.get(table).is_some_and(|owned| owned.contains(f))) {
    56	            return Ok(None);
    57	        }
    58	        self.tx.provisional_groups(table, fields)
    59	    }
    60	
    61	    // Recover a read's parent/group from the pinned index. A member point uses
    62	    // one ordinary-key lookup; a scan without the parent stays conservative.
    63	    pub(super) fn receipt_read_group(&mut self, m: &Maintain, index: &str, key: &[Value]) -> Result<Option<Vec<Value>>, RuntimeError> {
    64	        if !self.defer_receipt_maintain(m) { return Ok(None); }
    65	        let columns = self.schema.tables[&m.target].indexes[index].columns();
    66	        let pinned = |field: &str| columns.iter().position(|c| c == field).and_then(|i| key.get(i)).cloned();
    67	        if let Some(k) = &m.row_key {
    68	            if let Some(parent) = pinned(&k.member_key) { return Ok(Some(vec![parent])); }
    69	            if let Some(id) = pinned("member").or_else(|| pinned("id")) {
    70	                let row = self.metered_scan(|r, scope| r.tx.get(scope, &k.table, id.as_str()?))?;
    71	                return Ok(row.and_then(|row| row.field(&k.member_key).ok().cloned()).map(|v| vec![v]));
    72	            }
    73	            return Ok(None);
    74	        }
    75	        Ok(m.by.iter().map(|f| pinned(f)).collect())
    76	    }

    78	    pub(super) fn maintain(&mut self, source: &str, old: Option<&Value>, new: Option<&Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    79	        self.receipt_pending_groups.clear();
    80	        // Inserts ordinarily charge their actual aggregate work. A deferred
    81	        // predicate cannot select that work on a device, so use the same public
    82	        // worst-case reservation as update/delete before skipping it. Already
    83	        // prepaid writes must not pay twice.
    84	        if self.device && !self.maintain_prepaid && self.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|
    85	            m.source == source && m.private.is_none() && m.row_key.is_none() && (self.schema.receipt_dependencies.contains_key(&m.target) || self.schema.tables[&m.target].sync.is_none())) {
    86	            self.reserve_maintains(source, &self.schema.tables[source])?;
    87	        }
    88	        self.maintain_row_views(source, old, new)?;
    89	        let maintains = self.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|maintain| maintain.source == source && maintain.row_key.is_none());
    90	        for maintain in maintains {
    91	            if self.defer_receipt_maintain(maintain) || (self.device && self.schema.tables[&maintain.target].sync.is_none()) { continue; }
    92	            let source_bytes = super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(&self.schema.tables[&maintain.source]);
    93	            let target = &self.schema.tables[&maintain.target];
    94	            let bytes = source_bytes.saturating_mul(2).saturating_add(super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(target).saturating_mul(16 + 4 * target.indexes.len() as u64));
    95	            let prepaid = self.maintain_prepaid;
    96	            if maintain.private.is_some() && self.column_prepaid { self.maintain_prepaid = true; }
    97	            let result = self.maintain_scratch(bytes, |runner| runner.maintain_one(maintain, old, new));
    98	            self.maintain_prepaid = prepaid;
    99	            result?;
   100	        }
   101	        Ok(())
   102	    }
   103	
   104	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.9 — reserve interpreted images, both keys, targets and index buffers before allocation; release transient ownership on every exit.
   105	    pub(super) fn maintain_scratch<T>(&mut self, bytes: u64, work: impl FnOnce(&mut Self) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
   106	        if self.maintain_prepaid { return work(self); }
   107	        self.meter.heap(bytes)?;
   108	        let result = work(self);
   109	        self.meter.used.heap_bytes = self.meter.used.heap_bytes.saturating_sub(bytes);
   110	        result
   111	    }

   113	    fn maintain_one(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, old: Option<&Value>, new: Option<&Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   114	        let table = self.schema.table(&maintain.source).expect("validated");
   115	        let mut changes: Vec<(Vec<Value>, i128)> = Vec::new();
   116	        for (image, direction) in [(old, -1), (new, 1)] {
   117	            let Some(image) = image else { continue };
   118	            self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Steps, maintain.evaluation_steps())?;
   119	            let image = crate::schema::row_image(image, table);
   120	            if !maintain.selected(&image) { continue; }
   121	            let key = maintain.source_key(&image);
   122	            let change = delta(maintain, &image, direction)?;
   123	            if let Some(existing) = changes.iter_mut().find(|(k, _)| *k == key) { existing.1 += change; }
   124	            else { changes.push((key, change)); }
   125	        }
   126	        for (key, change) in changes {
   127	            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   128	            if let Some(retiring) = self.retiring.as_mut() {
   129	                let entry = retiring.aggregates.entry((maintain.target.clone(), canonical_json(&key))).or_insert_with(|| (key, 0));
   130	                entry.1 = entry.1.checked_add(change).ok_or_else(|| aggregate_overflow(maintain))?;
   131	                continue;
   132	            }
   133	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {
   134	                self.apply_aggregate(maintain, key, change, None)?;
   135	            }
   136	        }
   137	        Ok(())
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    /// Apply a retirement's net aggregate changes, one per target row, in
   141	    /// key order. Only a total the commit really ends with can overflow.
   142	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   143	    pub(super) fn apply_retired_aggregates(&mut self, changes: BTreeMap<(String, String), (Vec<Value>, i128)>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   144	        let schema = self.schema;
   145	        for ((target, _), (key, change)) in changes {
   146	            let maintain = schema.maintain_for(&target).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing maintain for {target}")))?;
   147	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {
   148	                self.apply_aggregate(maintain, key, change, None)?;
   149	            }
   150	        }
   151	        Ok(())
   152	    }
   153	
   154	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   155	    pub(crate) fn rebuild_maintain_reported(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, report: &mut crate::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   156	        let started = std::time::Instant::now();
   157	        self.rebuilt_source_rows = 0;
   158	        self.rebuild_maintain(maintain)?;
   159	        let rows = self.rebuilt_source_rows;
   160	        report.views.push(crate::store::adoption_report::ViewBuild { name: maintain.label(), rows, elapsed: started.elapsed() });
   161	        Ok(())
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    /// Build one aggregate from the rows in the store: the target's rows go,
   165	    /// then every source row contributes once. A schema change that adds or
   166	    /// redefines a `maintain` calls this, so a count over existing rows is
   167	    /// right from its first read (day two, LLP 2000.000 §11.2).
   168	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   169	    pub(crate) fn rebuild_maintain(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   170	        let heap = self.meter.used.heap_bytes;
   171	        let saved = self.column_rebuild;
   172	        self.column_rebuild |= maintain.private.is_some();
   173	        let result = self.rebuild_maintain_inner(maintain);
   174	        self.column_rebuild = saved;
   175	        self.meter.used.heap_bytes = heap;
   176	        result
   177	    }
   178	
   179	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   180	    fn rebuild_maintain_inner(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   181	        if maintain.row_key.is_some() { return self.rebuild_row_view(maintain); }
   182	        let target = self.schema.table(&maintain.target).expect("validated");
   183	        let mut bounds = Bounds::default();
   184	        let target_bytes = super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(target).saturating_mul(16 + 4 * target.indexes.len() as u64);
   185	        self.meter.heap(target_bytes.saturating_mul(256))?;
   186	        loop {
   187	            let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &maintain.target, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   188	            if page.is_empty() { break; }
   189	            for (existing, key) in page {
   190	                bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&key);
   191	                self.refuse_timeline_write(&maintain.target, crate::schema::Effect::Delete)?;
   192	                let entries = self.tx.delete(&maintain.target, target, &existing)?;
   193	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::IndexEntries, entries)?;
   194	                self.row_changed(&maintain.target, Some(&existing), None, Origin::Maintained)?;
   195	            }
   196	        }
   197	        let source = self.schema.table(&maintain.source).expect("validated");
   198	        self.meter.heap(super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(source).saturating_mul(256))?;
   199	        let mut totals: BTreeMap<String, (Vec<Value>, i128)> = BTreeMap::new();
   200	        bounds = Bounds::default();
   201	        loop {
   202	            let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &maintain.source, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   203	            if page.is_empty() { break; }
   204	            for (mut image, key) in page {
   205	                self.rebuilt_source_rows += 1;
   206	                bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&key);
   207	                materialize_optional_fields(&mut image, source);
   208	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   209	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Steps, maintain.evaluation_steps())?;
   210	                if !maintain.selected(&image) { continue; }
   211	                // @ref LLP 3000.028#6 — a total unique owner key has one contribution; backfill streams bounded pages instead of retaining all lifetime keys in a totals map.
   212	                if maintain.own_keys.is_some() {
   213	                    let heap = self.meter.used.heap_bytes;
   214	                    self.meter.heap(target_bytes)?;
   215	                    self.apply_aggregate(maintain, maintain.source_key(&image), 1, Some(true))?;
   216	                    self.meter.used.heap_bytes = heap;
   217	                    continue;
   218	                }
   219	                self.meter.heap(target_bytes)?;
   220	                let key = maintain.source_key(&image);
   221	                let change = delta(maintain, &image, 1)?;
   222	                let entry = match totals.entry(canonical_json(&key)) {
   223	                    std::collections::btree_map::Entry::Occupied(entry) => {
   224	                        self.meter.used.heap_bytes -= target_bytes;
   225	                        entry.into_mut()
   226	                    }
   227	                    std::collections::btree_map::Entry::Vacant(entry) => entry.insert((key, 0)),
   228	                };
   229	                entry.1 = entry.1.checked_add(change).ok_or_else(|| aggregate_overflow(maintain))?;
   230	            }
   231	        }
   232	        for (_, (key, change)) in totals {
   233	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {
   234	                self.apply_aggregate(maintain, key, change, Some(true))?;
   235	            }
   236	        }
   237	        Ok(())
   238	    }
   239	
   240	    fn live_sum(&self, maintain: &Maintain) -> bool {
   241	        matches!(maintain.kind, MaintainKind::Sum { .. }) && self.schema.table(&maintain.source).is_some_and(|source| source.is_expiring())
   242	    }
   243	
   244	    /// A zero sum is still a group while source rows remain: one signed
   245	    /// contribution may expire before the other. Keep its candidate row so
   246	    /// a scan, snapshot and offline replica can discover the later live sum.
   247	    fn aggregate_source_present(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: &[Value]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {

commit 4156cdf7e56fa066b8e206d9e49945dac23ab48f
Author: Charlie Cheever <ccheever@firewoodagent.com>
Date:   Thu Oct 15 00:35:56 2026 +0200

    Add cross-runtime Wave 12 language evidence

diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-device/tests/wave12_language.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-device/tests/wave12_language.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..8ca0e0832a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-device/tests/wave12_language.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,84 @@
+//! Wave 12 language parity on the web-memory and SQLite device stores.
+mod common;
+use common::{Host, Memory, Sqlite, compile, id, snapshot_pages, stream_page};
+use serde_json::json;
+use snapback4_core::{Context, Value, WriteState};
+
+const SOURCE: &str = r#"
+table entries:
+  category: enum('coffee-shop', 'book_store', 'museum')
+  contact: text 3..254 format email
+  amount: decimal 2
+  search category
+  public 'wave 12 device fixture'
+  insert <- allow
+  sync public
+view total: count entries
+mutation add(id: id, category: enum('coffee-shop', 'book_store', 'museum'), contact: text 3..254 format email, amount: decimal 2):
+  return insert entries { id, category, contact, amount }
+query totalEntries():
+  return total
+query search(q: text <=40, c: cursor ?):
+  return entries matching q first 20 by .id after c
+query extrema():
+  n = count(entries)
+  require n > 0 else EMPTY
+  return { low: min(entries, .amount), high: max(entries, .amount), sum: sum(entries, .amount) }
+query rounded(value: decimal 2):
+  return [divide(value, 2, 'half-even'), divide(value, 2, 'half-away-from-zero')]
+query average(total: int, count: int):
+  return divide(total, count, 2, 'half-even')
+query email(value: text 3..254 format email):
+  return value
+"#;
+
+fn parity<H: Host>(host: &mut H) {
+    let (mut server, backend) = compile(SOURCE);
+    server.seed([
+        ("entries".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
+            ("id", id("a")), ("category", Value::String("coffee-shop".into())),
+            ("contact", Value::String("a@example.com".into())), ("amount", Value::Int(5)),
+        ])),
+        ("entries".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
+            ("id", id("b")), ("category", Value::String("book_store".into())),
+            ("contact", Value::String("b@example.com".into())), ("amount", Value::Int(-5)),
+        ])),
+    ]).unwrap();
+    let mut device = host.open("alice", Some(backend)).unwrap();
+    let pages = snapshot_pages(&mut server, "alice", 100);
+    for (index, page) in pages.iter().enumerate() { device.apply(page, index == 0).unwrap(); }
+
+    assert_eq!(device.query("totalEntries", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(2));
+    assert_eq!(device.query("extrema", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data,
+        snapback4_core::value::object([("low", Value::Int(-5)), ("high", Value::Int(5)), ("sum", Value::Int(0))]));
+    assert_eq!(device.query("rounded", "alice", &json!({"value":5}), 10).unwrap().data,
+        Value::Array(vec![Value::Int(2), Value::Int(3)]));
+    assert_eq!(device.query("average", "alice", &json!({"total":101,"count":2}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(5050));
+    assert_eq!(device.query("average", "alice", &json!({"total":-1,"count":8}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(-12));
+    assert_eq!(device.query("email", "alice", &json!({"value":"a@localhost"}), 10).unwrap().data,
+        Value::String("a@localhost".into()));
+    assert_eq!(device.query("email", "alice", &json!({"value":"a..b@example.com"}), 10).unwrap_err().code, "E_INPUT");
+    let hits = device.query("search", "alice", &json!({"q":"cof","c":null}), 10).unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(hits.data.as_array().unwrap().len(), 1);
+    assert_eq!(hits.data.as_array().unwrap()[0].field("id").unwrap(), &id("a"));
+
+    let watermark = pages.last().unwrap().watermark;
+    let result = server.mutate("add", None, Context::new("alice", [
+        ("id".into(), id("c")), ("category".into(), Value::String("museum".into())),
+        ("contact".into(), Value::String("c@example.com".into())), ("amount".into(), Value::Int(9)),
+    ].into_iter().collect())).unwrap();
+    assert!(matches!(result.state, WriteState::Sent { .. }), "{result:?}");
+    device.apply(&stream_page(&mut server, "alice", watermark), false).unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(device.query("totalEntries", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(3));
+    host.close("alice", device);
+    let mut reopened = host.open("alice", None).unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(reopened.query("totalEntries", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(3),
+        "the compiler-owned global discriminator survives store reopen");
+    host.close("alice", reopened);
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn wave12_semantics_match_on_both_device_stores() {
+    parity(&mut Memory::new());
+    parity(&mut Sqlite::new("wave12-language"));
+}

+                Some(scale) => format!("divide({}, {}, {scale}, '{}')", dividend.source(), divisor.source(), rounding.source()),
+                None => format!("divide({}, {}, '{}')", dividend.source(), divisor.source(), rounding.source()),
+            },

     }
     pub fn simpler(&self) -> Vec<Self> {
@@ -309,6 +337,10 @@ impl Expr {

+                for e in b.simpler() { out.push(Self::RoundedDivide(rounding.clone(), *scale, a.clone(), Box::new(e))); }

+/// Exact rounded division comes from its own stream, preserving every older
+/// case whose stream does not select the new expression.
+fn rounded_division(case: &mut Case) {
+    let mut rng = Rng::new(case.seed ^ ROUND_DIV_STREAM);
+    if rng.pick(6) != 0 || !matches!(case.program.body, Body::Scalar(_)) { return; }
+    let modes = [Rounding::TowardZero, Rounding::AwayFromZero, Rounding::Floor,
+        Rounding::Ceiling, Rounding::HalfAwayFromZero, Rounding::HalfEven];
+    let dividend = if rng.pick(2) == 0 { Expr::Arg("a".into(), Type::Int) }
+        else { Expr::Int([-7, -5, -1, 0, 1, 5, 7][rng.pick(7) as usize]) };
+    let divisor = if rng.pick(2) == 0 { Expr::Arg("b".into(), Type::Int) }
+        else { Expr::Int([-3, -2, -1, 0, 1, 2, 3][rng.pick(7) as usize]) };
+    let scale = match rng.pick(5) { 0 => None, 1 => Some(0), 2 => Some(2), 3 => Some(6), _ => Some(12) };
+    case.program.body = Body::Scalar(Expr::RoundedDivide(
+        modes[rng.pick(modes.len() as u64) as usize].clone(), scale, Box::new(dividend), Box::new(divisor)));
+}
+

 fn timed_disclosure(case: &mut Case) {

diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-sim/tests/wave12_language.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-sim/tests/wave12_language.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..594f6cfbf1
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-sim/tests/wave12_language.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,99 @@
+//! Wave 12 language surfaces through the real server engine.
+use snapback4_core::{Context, Engine, Value, WriteState};
+use std::collections::BTreeMap;
+
+const SOURCE: &str = r#"
+table entries:
+  category: enum('coffee-shop', 'book_store', 'museum')
+  contact: text 3..254 format email
+  amount: decimal 2
+  search category
+  public 'wave 12 fixture'
+  insert <- allow
+  delete <- allow
+  online only
+table staff:
+  principal: principal
+  role: enum('finance')
+  unique principal, role
+  read <- .principal = viewer
+  online only
+view total: count entries
+  online only
+view categoryTotals: count entries by .category
+  read <- exists staff[principal = viewer, role = 'finance']
+  online only
+mutation add(id: id, category: enum('coffee-shop', 'book_store', 'museum'), contact: text 3..254 format email, amount: decimal 2):
+  return insert entries { id, category, contact, amount }
+mutation remove(id: entries):
+  delete entries[id]
+  return null
+query totalEntries():
+  return total
+query categoryTotal(category: enum('coffee-shop', 'book_store', 'museum')):
+  return categoryTotals[category]
+query guardedCategoryTotal(category: enum('coffee-shop', 'book_store', 'museum')):
+  require exists staff[principal = viewer, role = 'finance'] else NOT_FINANCE
+  return categoryTotals[category] + 1
+query search(q: text <=40, c: cursor ?):
+  return entries matching q first 20 by .id after c
+query extrema():
+  n = count(entries)
+  require n > 0 else EMPTY
+  return { low: min(entries, .amount), high: max(entries, .amount), sum: sum(entries, .amount) }
+query rounded(value: decimal 2):
+  return [divide(value, 2, 'half-even'), divide(value, 2, 'half-away-from-zero')]
+query average(total: int, count: int):
+  return divide(total, count, 2, 'half-even')
+query email(value: text 3..254 format email):
+  return value
+"#;
+
+fn args(values: &[(&str, Value)]) -> BTreeMap<String, Value> {
+    values.iter().map(|(name, value)| ((*name).into(), value.clone())).collect()
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn server_executes_all_wave12_semantic_surfaces_together() {
+    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), SOURCE.into())]);
+    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
+    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|program| program.program)).unwrap();
+    let call = |engine: &mut Engine, name, values: &[(&str, Value)]| {
+        engine.query(name, Context::new("dev:alice", args(values))).unwrap().data
+    };
+
+    assert_eq!(call(&mut engine, "totalEntries", &[]), Value::Int(0));
+    for (id, category, amount) in [("a", "coffee-shop", 5), ("b", "book_store", -5)] {
+        let result = engine.mutate("add", None, Context::new("dev:alice", args(&[
+            ("id", Value::Id(id.into())),
+            ("category", Value::String(category.into())),
+            ("contact", Value::String(format!("{id}@example.com"))),
+            ("amount", Value::Int(amount)),
+        ]))).unwrap();
+        assert!(matches!(result.state, WriteState::Sent { .. }), "{result:?}");
+    }
+    assert_eq!(call(&mut engine, "totalEntries", &[]), Value::Int(2));
+    assert_eq!(call(&mut engine, "categoryTotal", &[("category", Value::String("coffee-shop".into()))]), Value::Null,
+        "a hidden keyed hit is indistinguishable from a hidden miss");
+    engine.seed([("staff".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
+        ("id", Value::Id("finance".into())), ("principal", Value::Id("dev:alice".into())),
+        ("role", Value::String("finance".into())),
+    ]))]).unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(call(&mut engine, "guardedCategoryTotal", &[("category", Value::String("coffee-shop".into()))]), Value::Int(2));
+    assert_eq!(call(&mut engine, "guardedCategoryTotal", &[("category", Value::String("museum".into()))]), Value::Int(1));
+    assert_eq!(call(&mut engine, "extrema", &[]), snapback4_core::value::object([
+        ("low", Value::Int(-5)), ("high", Value::Int(5)), ("sum", Value::Int(0)),
+    ]));
+    assert_eq!(call(&mut engine, "rounded", &[("value", Value::Int(5))]), Value::Array(vec![Value::Int(2), Value::Int(3)]));
+    assert_eq!(call(&mut engine, "average", &[("total", Value::Int(101)), ("count", Value::Int(2))]), Value::Int(5050));
+    assert_eq!(call(&mut engine, "average", &[("total", Value::Int(-1)), ("count", Value::Int(8))]), Value::Int(-12));
+    assert_eq!(call(&mut engine, "email", &[("value", Value::String("a@localhost".into()))]), Value::String("a@localhost".into()));
+    assert_eq!(engine.query("email", Context::new("dev:alice", args(&[("value", Value::String("a..b@example.com".into()))]))).unwrap_err().code, "E_INPUT");
+    let hits = call(&mut engine, "search", &[("q", Value::String("cof".into())), ("c", Value::Null)]);
+    assert_eq!(hits.as_array().unwrap().len(), 1);
+    assert_eq!(hits.as_array().unwrap()[0].field("id").unwrap(), &Value::Id("a".into()));
+
+    engine.mutate("remove", None, Context::new("dev:alice", args(&[("id", Value::Id("a".into()))]))).unwrap();
+    engine.mutate("remove", None, Context::new("dev:alice", args(&[("id", Value::Id("b".into()))]))).unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(call(&mut engine, "totalEntries", &[]), Value::Int(0));
+}

//! Null facts for repeated, effect-free helper calls over lexical snapshots.
use super::*;
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

pub(super) const CONSTANTS: &str = "$pureCallConstants";

#[derive(Debug)]
pub(super) enum Stable {
    Literal(String),
    Snapshot(String, Vec<String>),
    Viewer,
    Call(String, Vec<Stable>),
}

impl Binder<'_> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.2 — a repeated pure call narrows like a snapshot field.
    // Facts live on *every* argument root so the existing branch intersections,
    // reassignment invalidation and loop fixed point apply to them as to fields.
    fn stable_call(&self, value: &A) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
        if !matches!(value, A::Call { .. }) { return None; }
        let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
        let stable = self.stable_value(value, &mut roots)?;
        if roots.is_empty() { roots.insert(CONSTANTS.into()); }
        Some((vec!["$pureCall".into(), stable.key()], roots))
    }

    pub(super) fn stable_value(&self, value: &A, roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<Stable> {
        match value {
            A::Literal { .. } => Some(Stable::Literal(print::expression(value))),
            A::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer" => Some(Stable::Viewer),
            A::Name { name, .. } if matches!(name.as_str(), "now" | "commit" | "new") => None,
            A::Name { .. } | A::Member { .. } | A::Focal { .. } => {
                let (root, path) = control::field_path(value, self.focal.as_ref().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()))?;
                let runtime = if let Some(binding) = self.binding(&root) {
                    roots.insert(root.clone());
                    binding.runtime.clone()
                } else {
                    // A shape's implicit row is an immutable lexical snapshot.
                    // Its unique runtime identity separates projection scopes;
                    // with no mutable argument roots, use the constant anchor.
                    self.focal.as_ref().filter(|(name, _)| name == &root)?.0.clone()
                };
                Some(Stable::Snapshot(runtime, path))
            }
            A::Call { name, args, .. } => {
                let function = self.catalog.functions.get(name)?;
                if !self.pure_functions.get(&function.name).copied().unwrap_or(false) { return None; }
                Some(Stable::Call(function.name.clone(), args.iter().map(|arg| self.stable_value(arg, roots)).collect::<Option<_>>()?))
            }
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn narrow_call(&mut self, value: &A, present: bool) {

   144	    pub(super) fn scan_filter(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl) -> Fallible<(ReadExpr, Option<Box<Expr>>)> {

   210	    pub(super) fn row_predicate(&mut self, table: &str, predicate: &A) -> Fallible<Expr> {
   211	        let row_ty = Ty::Row(table.into());
   212	        let prior = self.focal.replace(("$filter".into(), row_ty.clone()));
   213	        let kind = std::mem::replace(&mut self.kind, ProgramKind::Query);
   214	        let predicate_context = std::mem::replace(&mut self.in_predicate, true);
   215	        let mut body = setup::Body::default();
   216	        self.push_scope();
   217	        // A fresh lexical row snapshot lets short-circuit guards narrow its
   218	        // fields without leaking facts to another scan or an enclosing row.
   219	        self.scopes.last_mut().unwrap().insert("$filter".into(), Binding {
   220	            runtime: "$filter".into(), declared: row_ty.clone(), ty: row_ty,
   221	            parameter: false, revision: 0, fields: BTreeMap::new(), error: None, origins: Vec::new(),
   222	        });
   223	        let bound = self.expr(predicate, &mut body);
   224	        self.pop_scope();
   225	        self.kind = kind;
   226	        self.in_predicate = predicate_context;
   227	        self.focal = prior;
   228	        let (value, ty) = bound?;
   229	        if !ty.is_bool() { return Err(type_error(predicate.site(), format!("where needs a bool predicate, got {}", ty.name()), "where .col = value")); }
   230	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.6 — predicate setup, including lazy reads, runs afresh for each rule-visible row.
   231	        Ok(if body.is_empty() { value } else { Expr::Block { body: body.code, value: Box::new(value) } })
   232	    }
   233	}
   234	
   235	fn pins_leading_column(expr: &A, column: Option<&str>) -> bool {
   236	    let focal_column = |value: &A| matches!(value, A::Member { base, name, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. }) && Some(name.as_str()) == column);
   237	    match expr {
   238	        A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" => pins_leading_column(left, column) || pins_leading_column(right, column),
   239	        A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => pins_leading_column(left, column) && pins_leading_column(right, column),
   240	        A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "=" => (focal_column(left) && !has_focal(right)) || (focal_column(right) && !has_focal(left)),
   241	        _ => false,
   242	    }
   243	}

   245	fn has_focal(expr: &A) -> bool {

     1	//! Wave 12 language parity on the web-memory and SQLite device stores.
     2	mod common;
     3	use common::{Host, Memory, Sqlite, compile, id, snapshot_pages, stream_page};
     4	use serde_json::json;
     5	use snapback4_core::{Context, Value, WriteState};
     6	
     7	const SOURCE: &str = r#"
     8	table entries:
     9	  category: enum('coffee-shop', 'book_store', 'museum')
    10	  contact: text 3..254 format email
    11	  amount: decimal 2
    12	  search category
    13	  public 'wave 12 device fixture'
    14	  insert <- allow
    15	  sync public
    16	view total: count entries
    17	mutation add(id: id, category: enum('coffee-shop', 'book_store', 'museum'), contact: text 3..254 format email, amount: decimal 2):
    18	  return insert entries { id, category, contact, amount }
    19	query totalEntries():
    20	  return total
    21	query search(q: text <=40, c: cursor ?):
    22	  return entries matching q first 20 by .id after c
    23	query extrema():
    24	  n = count(entries)
    25	  require n > 0 else EMPTY
    26	  return { low: min(entries, .amount), high: max(entries, .amount), sum: sum(entries, .amount) }
    27	query rounded(value: decimal 2):
    28	  return [divide(value, 2, 'half-even'), divide(value, 2, 'half-away-from-zero')]
    29	query average(total: int, count: int):
    30	  return divide(total, count, 2, 'half-even')
    31	query email(value: text 3..254 format email):
    32	  return value
    33	"#;
    34	
    35	fn parity<H: Host>(host: &mut H) {
    36	    let (mut server, backend) = compile(SOURCE);
    37	    server.seed([
    38	        ("entries".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
    39	            ("id", id("a")), ("category", Value::String("coffee-shop".into())),
    40	            ("contact", Value::String("a@example.com".into())), ("amount", Value::Int(5)),
    41	        ])),
    42	        ("entries".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
    43	            ("id", id("b")), ("category", Value::String("book_store".into())),
    44	            ("contact", Value::String("b@example.com".into())), ("amount", Value::Int(-5)),
    45	        ])),
    46	    ]).unwrap();
    47	    let mut device = host.open("alice", Some(backend)).unwrap();
    48	    let pages = snapshot_pages(&mut server, "alice", 100);
    49	    for (index, page) in pages.iter().enumerate() { device.apply(page, index == 0).unwrap(); }
    50	
    51	    assert_eq!(device.query("totalEntries", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(2));
    52	    assert_eq!(device.query("extrema", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data,
    53	        snapback4_core::value::object([("low", Value::Int(-5)), ("high", Value::Int(5)), ("sum", Value::Int(0))]));
    54	    assert_eq!(device.query("rounded", "alice", &json!({"value":5}), 10).unwrap().data,
    55	        Value::Array(vec![Value::Int(2), Value::Int(3)]));
    56	    assert_eq!(device.query("average", "alice", &json!({"total":101,"count":2}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(5050));
    57	    assert_eq!(device.query("average", "alice", &json!({"total":-1,"count":8}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(-12));
    58	    assert_eq!(device.query("email", "alice", &json!({"value":"a@localhost"}), 10).unwrap().data,
    59	        Value::String("a@localhost".into()));
    60	    assert_eq!(device.query("email", "alice", &json!({"value":"a..b@example.com"}), 10).unwrap_err().code, "E_INPUT");
    61	    let hits = device.query("search", "alice", &json!({"q":"cof","c":null}), 10).unwrap();
    62	    assert_eq!(hits.data.as_array().unwrap().len(), 1);
    63	    assert_eq!(hits.data.as_array().unwrap()[0].field("id").unwrap(), &id("a"));
    64	
    65	    let watermark = pages.last().unwrap().watermark;
    66	    let result = server.mutate("add", None, Context::new("alice", [
    67	        ("id".into(), id("c")), ("category".into(), Value::String("museum".into())),
    68	        ("contact".into(), Value::String("c@example.com".into())), ("amount".into(), Value::Int(9)),
    69	    ].into_iter().collect())).unwrap();
    70	    assert!(matches!(result.state, WriteState::Sent { .. }), "{result:?}");
    71	    device.apply(&stream_page(&mut server, "alice", watermark), false).unwrap();
    72	    assert_eq!(device.query("totalEntries", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(3));
    73	    host.close("alice", device);
    74	    let mut reopened = host.open("alice", None).unwrap();
    75	    assert_eq!(reopened.query("totalEntries", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(3),
    76	        "the compiler-owned global discriminator survives store reopen");
    77	    host.close("alice", reopened);
    78	}
    79	
    80	#[test]
    81	fn wave12_semantics_match_on_both_device_stores() {
    82	    parity(&mut Memory::new());
    83	    parity(&mut Sqlite::new("wave12-language"));
    84	}

+/-- Truncating quotient/remainder followed by one exact rounding step. The
+model deliberately derives truncation from the existing Euclidean primitive,
+so its oracle is independent of the Rust implementation. -/
+def roundedDiv (rounding : Rounding) (scale : Option Nat) (x y : Int) : EM Value := do
+  if y = 0 then refuse "E_DIV_ZERO"
+  let x := x * (10 : Int) ^ scale.getD 0
+  let qe := Int.ediv x y
+  let re := Int.emod x y
+  let q := if x < 0 ∧ re ≠ 0 then qe + (if y < 0 then -1 else 1) else qe
+  let r := x - q * y
+  if r = 0 then checkI53 q else
+    let direction : Int := if (x < 0) = (y < 0) then 1 else -1
+    let away := q + direction
+    let result := match rounding with
+      | .towardZero => q
+      | .awayFromZero => away
+      | .floor => if direction < 0 then away else q
+      | .ceiling => if 0 < direction then away else q
+      | .halfAwayFromZero => if Int.natAbs r * 2 ≥ Int.natAbs y then away else q
+      | .halfEven =>
+          if Int.natAbs r * 2 > Int.natAbs y ∨
+              (Int.natAbs r * 2 = Int.natAbs y ∧ Int.emod q 2 ≠ 0)
+          then away else q
+    checkI53 result
+

 non-positive period or an offset outside `[0, period)` is unreachable from a
 checked program. -/
@@ -930,6 +955,12 @@ def evalExpr : Nat → Ctx → View → Env → Option Value → Expr → EM (Va

         | none => typeErr
       | _, _ => typeErr
+    | .roundDiv rounding scale a b => do
+      let (x, _) ← ev a
+      let (y, _) ← ev b
+      match x, y with
+      | .s (.int x), .s (.int y) => return (← roundedDiv rounding scale x y, none)

diff --git a/snapback4/lean/Quarry/Wave12.lean b/snapback4/lean/Quarry/Wave12.lean
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..b8a81fddb8
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/lean/Quarry/Wave12.lean
@@ -0,0 +1,146 @@
+/-!
+# Wave 12 semantic witnesses
+
+These executable definitions cover language contracts outside the row IR:
+global count visibility, capture-time count provenance, email validation, and
+enum values entering the existing word-search path. Rounded division lives in
+`Quarry.Eval` because it is an IR builtin.
+-/
+
+namespace Quarry.Wave12
+
+def globalCount (rows : List α) : Nat := rows.length
+
+@[simp] theorem globalCount_empty : globalCount ([] : List α) = 0 := rfl
+
+theorem positive_count_has_member {rows : List α} (h : 0 < globalCount rows) :
+    ∃ row, row ∈ rows := by
+  cases rows with
+  | nil => simp [globalCount] at h
+  | cons row rows => exact ⟨row, by simp⟩
+
+/-- A readable absent global count is zero; hidden hit and miss are both
+`none`, so absence never becomes a disclosure channel. -/
+def visibleGlobalCount (authorized : Bool) (stored : Option Nat) : Option Nat :=
+  if authorized then some (stored.getD 0) else none
+
+example : visibleGlobalCount true none = some 0 := rfl
+example : visibleGlobalCount false none = none := rfl
+example : visibleGlobalCount false (some 7) = none := rfl
+
+/-- Binder provenance is a capture-time domain, transaction and authority
+triple. A retained diagnostic origin is intentionally not part of it. -/
+structure CountCapture where
+  domain : String
+  transaction : Nat
+  authority : String
+  deriving DecidableEq
+
+def countCaptureLive (capture : CountCapture) (domain : String)
+    (transaction : Nat) (authority : String) : Bool :=
+  capture.domain == domain && capture.transaction == transaction &&
+    capture.authority == authority
+
+example : countCaptureLive ⟨"entries/owner=alice", 4, "alice"⟩
+    "entries/owner=alice" 4 "alice" = true := rfl
+example : countCaptureLive ⟨"entries/owner=alice", 4, "alice"⟩
+    "entries/owner=alice" 5 "alice" = false := rfl
+example : countCaptureLive ⟨"entries/owner=alice", 4, "alice"⟩
+    "entries/owner=bob" 4 "alice" = false := rfl
+example : countCaptureLive ⟨"entries/owner=alice", 4, "alice"⟩
+    "entries/owner=alice" 4 "bob" = false := rfl
+
+/-- A keyed maintained count becomes required only for the exact key whose
+role predicate was proved in the same transaction. A readable miss is zero;
+hidden hits and misses share `none`. -/
+def keyedTotalAfterRoleGuard (guardKey readKey : String)
+    (sameTransaction authorized : Bool) (stored : Option Nat) : Option Nat :=
+  if sameTransaction && authorized && guardKey == readKey
+    then some (stored.getD 0)
+    else none
+
+example : keyedTotalAfterRoleGuard "dept-a" "dept-a" true true none = some 0 := rfl
+example : keyedTotalAfterRoleGuard "dept-a" "dept-b" true true (some 7) = none := rfl
+example : keyedTotalAfterRoleGuard "dept-a" "dept-a" false true (some 7) = none := rfl
+example : keyedTotalAfterRoleGuard "dept-a" "dept-a" true false (some 7) = none := rfl
+
+def asciiAlphaNum (c : Char) : Bool :=
+  let n := c.toNat
+  (65 ≤ n && n ≤ 90) || (97 ≤ n && n ≤ 122) || (48 ≤ n && n ≤ 57)
+
+def asciiLower (c : Char) : Char :=
+  if 65 ≤ c.toNat && c.toNat ≤ 90 then Char.ofNat (c.toNat + 32) else c
+
+def splitOnce (needle : Char) : List Char → Option (List Char × List Char)
+  | [] => none
+  | c :: cs =>
+      if c == needle then some ([], cs) else
+        (splitOnce needle cs).map fun pair => (c :: pair.1, pair.2)
+
+def splitDots : List Char → List (List Char)
+  | [] => [[]]
+  | c :: cs =>
+      let rest := splitDots cs
+      if c == '.' then [] :: rest else
+        match rest with
+        | [] => [[c]]
+        | label :: labels => (c :: label) :: labels
+
+def noAdjacentDots : List Char → Bool
+  | '.' :: '.' :: _ => false
+  | _ :: cs => noAdjacentDots cs
+  | [] => true
+
+def localPunctuation : List Char :=
+  ['.', '!', '#', '$', '%', '&', '\'', '*', '+', '/', '=', '?', '^', '_', '`', '{', '|', '}', '~', '-']
+
+def localPartValid (part : List Char) : Bool :=
+  !part.isEmpty && part.length ≤ 64 && part.head? != some '.' &&
+    part.getLast? != some '.' && noAdjacentDots part &&
+    part.all fun c => asciiAlphaNum c || localPunctuation.contains c
+
+def domainLabelValid (label : List Char) : Bool :=
+  !label.isEmpty && label.length ≤ 63 &&
+    label.head?.any asciiAlphaNum && label.getLast?.any asciiAlphaNum &&
+    label.all fun c => asciiAlphaNum c || c == '-'
+
+def emailValid (email : String) : Bool :=
+  let chars := email.toList
+  chars.length ≤ 254 && chars.all (·.toNat ≤ 127) &&
+    chars.count '@' == 1 && match splitOnce '@' chars with
+      | some (localChars, domain) => localPartValid localChars &&
+          !domain.isEmpty && domain.length ≤ 253 && (splitDots domain).all domainLabelValid
+      | none => false
+
+example : emailValid "first.last+tag@sub.example" = true := by decide
+example : emailValid "x@localhost" = true := by decide
+example : emailValid "a..b@example.com" = false := by decide
+example : emailValid "a@-example.com" = false := by decide
+example : emailValid "a@пример.рф" = false := by decide
+
+/-- ASCII executable witness for enum spellings using the shared word-search
+shape: lowercase, alphanumeric token boundaries, and query-prefix matching.
+The Rust/device tests own NFC and the production Unicode tables. -/
+def asciiTokens (text : String) : List (List Char) :=
+  let rec go (remaining current : List Char) (tokens : List (List Char)) :=
+    match remaining with
+    | [] => (if current.isEmpty then tokens else current.reverse :: tokens).reverse
+    | c :: cs =>
+        if asciiAlphaNum c then go cs (asciiLower c :: current) tokens
+        else go cs [] (if current.isEmpty then tokens else current.reverse :: tokens)
+  go text.toList [] []
+
+def isPrefix : List Char → List Char → Bool
+  | [], _ => true
+  | _, [] => false
+  | p :: ps, w :: ws => p == w && isPrefix ps ws
+
+def enumMatchesAscii (value query : String) : Bool :=
+  let words := asciiTokens value
+  (asciiTokens query).all fun term => words.any fun word => isPrefix term word
+
+example : enumMatchesAscii "coffee-shop" "cof" = true := by decide
+example : enumMatchesAscii "book_store" "BOOK" = true := by decide
+example : enumMatchesAscii "museum" "music" = false := by decide
+
+end Quarry.Wave12
diff --git a/snapback4/lean/README.md b/snapback4/lean/README.md
index 28adb8d6cc..bdf8297499 100644
--- a/snapback4/lean/README.md
+++ b/snapback4/lean/README.md
@@ -54,6 +54,7 @@ elan.
 | Evaluator | `Quarry/Eval.lean` | `evalExpr` and `execStmts`: total and structural on fuel. The page walk, `complete` and `next`, counts and search `exists`; when a scan is decided (its pins, or a page guard: a grant or a pinned parent probe). Reads see only a `View` of visible rows. |
 | Writes | `Quarry/Write.lean` | Insert, upsert, update and delete, with rule checks and the documented refusal codes (`E_RULE`, `NOT_FOUND`, `E_CONFLICT`, `E_CONSTRAINT`, `E_REFERENCED`, application codes). A delete applies its references' `on delete` actions (LLP 3000.039 §3): the read prerequisite, the cascade closure, restrict probes outside it, set null, removal. Caps are carried as unenforced declarations: the emitter admits a capped table only when the program never inserts into or updates it. |
 | Calendar | `Quarry/Calendar.lean` | The UTC builtins of LLP 3000.040 §2.2: `day`, `hour`, `bucket` as period ordinals; `month`, `year`, `monthStart`, `yearStart` over the proleptic Gregorian calendar, by a 400/100/4/1-year decomposition independent of the engine's algorithm. Unbounded `Int`; the evaluator refuses `E_OVERFLOW` outside i53. |
+| Wave 12 witnesses | `Quarry/Wave12.lean` | Independent executable contracts for readable-zero versus hidden global counts, positive-count population, capture-time domain/transaction/authority provenance, exact keyed-role narrowing/invalidation, the ASCII email grammar, and the ASCII fragment of enum word-prefix search. Production NFC/token limits remain Rust/device evidence. |
 | Running | `Quarry/Run.lean` | `run : Case → Outcome`. An outcome is an answer (data, `complete`, `next`), unavailable, committed (result and store), or refused (code). |

# Wave 12 language-lane status

Updated: 2026-10-15

## Scope

This lane owns the five semantic gaps: ungrouped maintained totals, sound
aggregate and maintained-result narrowing, email text format, enum search,
and exact rounded decimal division. It does not own engine expiry,
`maintained_id`, parser recovery, reserved-name diagnostics or the branch
planner.

## Progress

- Governing LLPs, guide cards, wave evidence lanes 019/021/027/083/091/093,
  NA10 and the pattern ledger read.
- Baseline gate `/tmp/lang-baseline-ready` observed before production edits.
- LLP 3000.013.001 records the semantic and verification contract and is
  linked into `llp/current/`.
- Email schema/runtime/client support landed in `b081f96054`.
- The remaining semantic foundation landed in `0fe7cc1263`: ungrouped global
  maintained counts, enum search, exact three/four-argument rounded division,
  and capture-time count provenance with stale-proof invalidation.
- Cross-runtime evidence landed in `4156cdf7e5`: generated rounded-division
  cases execute through native, Lean and both device stores; simulator and
  device scenarios exercise all five surfaces; standalone Lean witnesses
  cover the semantic fragments outside the row evaluator.
- The shared Lean lowering compile blocker reported in the coordination inbox
  is repaired; both Wave 12 language test executables compile again.
- Affected language tests pass 6/6. Simulator, difftest generation, memory
  device and SQLite device tests pass 1/1 each. Lean passes 54 build jobs, 65
  audited theorems, 88/88 examples and 7/7 fixtures. The focused CLI guide
  lookup regression passes, and `ref-check --verbose` passes.
- The full guide test currently passes 120/123. Its three residual failures
  are in concurrent, uncommitted diagnostics documentation: the maintained
  disclosure example names an undeclared table and exceeds its card budget,
  a personas block duplicates the project identity declaration, and their
  added prose moves the corpus-wide reduction ratio. Wave 12's modified guide
  examples, compiled-block preservation, language execution and email
  expectation all pass in that same run.
- Guide/spec commit and the two fresh blind READY reviews are in progress.

## Concerns / decisions

- Count non-emptiness is an exact transaction fact, never a general theorem
  prover; conservative misses are acceptable, stale acceptance is not.
- The intermediate census's owner-or-manager structural-fanout refusals are
  in the planner/diagnostics lanes: this lane does not change OR-role planning.
- Hidden maintained misses remain null. Zero is produced only after an
  authorization proof.
- Rounded division uses exact signed integer quotient/remainder and an
  explicit enum mode. The four-argument integer form also names result scale,
  so fractional averages need no manual fixed-point multiply; no floating
  point or implicit `avg` is introduced.
